done

All 147 data units on the worklist that T7 left open now have a disposition, recorded in config/names_worklist.tsv. No data rows remain undisposed; the 326 rows still open are all functions.
- **Best guesses: 99.** Each has a `guess_` name with the code fact it rests on, its confidence and its evidence kind in config/names.tsv. By evidence: 67 rest on an already-named neighbour, 28 on the code alone, 4 on game strings. By confidence: 11 high, 65 medium, 23 low.
- **Left unnamed: 48.** Each has its reason recorded. Most are positions inside a record or array whose role the code doesn't show.
- **Cited: 0.** No unit had an outside record good enough to cite. The only live-verified data were already named in T7.
- **One extra cited name:** the PsyQ library's GsWSMATRIX (D_800AE688), which you approved at T7's review. Main still links byte-identical with it.

The work went in five batches of about 30, grouped by address so that neighbouring fields of one block got one family of names (menu state, camera records, HUD, target-time sprites, render work block). Each batch passed the batch procedure. Renames left the type census unchanged every time. At the last batch commit (948e2daef0), R22 is 218/218 and `make tools-health` is OK. At HEAD, `rename_scan --ledger --gate-citations` passes with 0 invalid rows, 0 guesses without a basis, 0 dead rows and 0 uncited curated names.

Three things went off course along the way:
- **Restart:** the session restart cut off the fourth coder before it committed. The next coder re-checked the sheet it left and found two false claims in the bases, which it corrected before applying the batch.
- **Batch 5 broke the build once:** main's own C reads six of the resident camera words. Putting their names in config/symbols.us.txt broke 212 overlay extracts, because every overlay also loads that file. The fix was to add them in config/symbols.us.ram.txt, which only main reads; that is the green commit 948e2daef0.
- **Wrong commit line:** the first batch's commit message gives the wrong counts. The summary has the right ones.

Two decisions I made inside the conventions:
- Names backed by game strings (villager names, prompt texts) stay as guesses: the bytes show the content, but the role word in the name is ours.
- Interior array elements stay unnamed, including the villager panel's cursor.

I banked two new cookbook entries:
- **C0573:** before the rename scan, write the new name beside lines that mention a datum only by its address, and include the kit-corpus mirror files in the commit.
- **C0574:** resident data that main's code uses gets its name in config/symbols.us.ram.txt.

Summary: /home/musashi/bfm-decomp/phase-ends/current/tasks/T8.md. Log: /home/musashi/bfm-decomp/phase-ends/current/logs/T8.md. Both are committed in daae47c955.
